# Skills · E · part 100

840 entries, explore to expo. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **explore**: Explores unfamiliar codebases, diagnoses problems, and provides contextual insights. Analyzes project structure, traces code logic, identifies issues, and generates actionable recommendations. Should be used when the user needs to explore or investigate a codebase — understanding project structure, diagnosing bugs, tracing feature implementation, or assessing code quality. Distinguished from context-learning which focuses on tracing specific code references, this skill focuses on project-wide exploration and diagnosis. ([ImaginerLabs/skill-manager](https://github.com/ImaginerLabs/skill-manager/tree/HEAD/skills/coding/explore))
